Method: DCE包含三种机制：1) 语言化尾部采样（模型标注想法明显程度，丢弃明显想法）；2) 语义记忆（维护持久嵌入索引拒绝近重复）；3) 自适应提示演化（基于记忆状态和多样性策略重构生成提示）。

Result: 在三个领域（可持续包装概念、教育考试题目、创意写作提示）和两个模型家族（gpt-5-mini和claude-haiku-4-5）的实验中，DCE实现0.0%崩溃率（vs 朴素提示5.6%），产生17-18个聚类（vs 2-17个），表明更丰富的概念结构。结果经独立嵌入模型验证，对参数变化稳健。

Conclusion: DCE有效解决跨批次模式崩溃问题，去重和提示演化单独不足但联合有效，成本约$0.50/千候选，无需微调或定制架构，为保持LLM输出多样性提供系统化框架。

Abstract: Large language models produce repetitive output when prompted independently across many batches, a phenomenon we term cross-batch mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity when a language model is prompted repeatedly without access to its prior generations. Practitioners have long mitigated this with ad hoc deduplication and seed rotation, but no principled framework exists. We introduce Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE), comprising three mechanisms: (1) verbalized tail sampling (the model labels each idea with a guess about how obvious it is, and obvious ideas are discarded), which filters high-probability candidates via model self-assessment; (2) semantic memory, which maintains a persistent embedding index to reject near-duplicates across batches; and (3) adaptive prompt evolution, which reconstructs the generation prompt each batch using memory state and rotating diversity strategies. In experiments across three domains (sustainable packaging concepts, educational exam questions, and creative writing prompts) and two model families (gpt-5-mini and claude-haiku-4-5), a component ablation across 2-3 random seeds per method shows that DCE achieves 0.0 +/- 0.0% collapse versus 5.6 +/- 2.0% for naive prompting, while producing 17-18 HDBSCAN clusters per seed versus naive's volatile 2-17, indicating reliably richer conceptual structure. These results are validated with an independent embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) and hold across sensitivity sweeps of the VTS threshold tau and dedup threshold delta. Deduplication and prompt evolution are individually insufficient but jointly effective, at approximately $0.50 per 1,000 candidates using only standard API calls, with no fine-tuning or custom architectures required.

</details>


### [77] [DTCRS: Dynamic Tree Construction for Recursive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07012)
*Guanran Luo,Zhongquan Jian,Wentao Qiu,Meihong Wang,Qingqiang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DTCRS是一种动态生成摘要树的方法，通过分析问题类型决定是否需要摘要树，利用子问题嵌入作为初始聚类中心，减少冗余摘要并提高摘要与问题的相关性，显著降低构建时间并在三个QA任务上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统递归摘要方法存在两个主要问题：1）摘要树通常包含大量冗余摘要节点，增加构建时间并可能对问答产生负面影响；2）递归摘要并不适用于所有类型的问题。需要一种更智能的方法来动态决定何时以及如何构建摘要树。

Method: DTCRS（动态树构建递归摘要）方法：1）通过分析问题类型决定是否需要构建摘要树；2）将问题分解为子问题；3）使用子问题的嵌入向量作为初始聚类中心；4）基于文档结构和查询语义动态生成摘要树，减少冗余摘要并提高摘要与问题的相关性。

Result: DTCRS显著减少了摘要树构建时间，并在三个问答任务上取得了实质性改进。同时，研究还探讨了递归摘要对不同问题类型的适用性，为未来研究提供了有价值的见解。

Conclusion: DTCRS通过动态决定何时构建摘要树以及如何优化摘要树结构，有效解决了传统递归摘要方法的冗余问题和适用性限制，提高了检索增强生成系统的效率和准确性，为不同类型的问答任务提供了更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) mitigates the hallucination problem of Large Language Models (LLMs) by incorporating external knowledge. Recursive summarization constructs a hierarchical summary tree by clustering text chunks, integrating information from multiple parts of a document to provide evidence for abstractive questions involving multi-step reasoning. However, summary trees often contain a large number of redundant summary nodes, which not only increase construction time but may also negatively impact question answering. Moreover, recursive summarization is not suitable for all types of questions. We introduce DTCRS, a method that dynamically generates summary trees based on document structure and query semantics. DTCRS determines whether a summary tree is necessary by analyzing the question type. It then decomposes the question and uses the embeddings of sub-questions as initial cluster centers, reducing redundant summaries while improving the relevance between summaries and the question. Our approach significantly reduces summary tree construction time and achieves substantial improvements across three QA tasks. Additionally, we investigate the applicability of recursive summarization to different question types, providing valuable insights for future research.

</details>


### [78] [Corpora deduplication or duplication in Natural Language Processing of few resourced languages ? A case of study: The Mexico's Nahuatl](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07015)
*Juan-José Guzman-Landa,Juan-Manuel Torres-Moreno,Graham Ranger,Miguel Figueroa-Saavedra,Martha-Lorena Avendaño-Garrido,Elvys Linhares-Pontes,Luis-Gil Moreno-Jiménez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究数据重复对资源有限语言NLP是否有用，通过增量复制技术扩展Nawatl语料库，在语义相似度任务中获得了适度性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对计算资源有限的π语言（如Nawatl），可用训练语料稀少，探索数据重复是否能在NLP任务中带来益处

Method: 使用增量复制技术对π-yalli语料库进行受控扩展，训练静态词嵌入并在句子级语义相似度任务中评估

Result: 与未扩展语料相比，增量复制带来了适度的性能提升，且该方法在文献中尚未见报道

Conclusion: 数据重复对资源有限语言的NLP任务具有潜在价值，增量复制技术能有效改善模型性能

Abstract: In this article, we seek to answer the following question: could data duplication be useful in Natural Language Processing (NLP) for languages with limited computational resources? In this type of languages (or $π$-languages), corpora available for training Large Language Models are virtually non-existent. In particular, we will study the impact of corpora expansion in Nawatl, an agglutinative and polysynthetic $π$-language spoken by over 2 million people, with a large number of dialectal varieties. The aim is to expand the new $π$-yalli corpus, which contains a limited number of Nawatl texts, by duplicating it in a controlled way. In our experiments, we will use the incremental duplication technique. The aim is to learn embeddings that are well-suited to NLP tasks. Thus, static embeddings were trained and evaluated in a sentence-level semantic similarity task. Our results show a moderate improvement in performance when using incremental duplication compared to the results obtained using only the corpus without expansion. Furthermore, to our knowledge, this technique has not yet been used in the literature.

</details>


### [79] [Evaluating In-Context Translation with Synchronous Context-Free Grammar Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07320)
*Jackson Petty,Jaulie Goe,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在低资源语言翻译中面临挑战，研究通过形式语法任务评估LLMs从上下文语法描述中学习语言映射的能力，发现语法规模、句子长度、形态和书写差异显著影响翻译准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言缺乏大规模训练数据，但LLMs可能通过上下文中的语言描述（如教科书、词典）来学习翻译。本研究旨在分离和评估LLMs从形式语法描述中推断语言映射的能力，为低资源语言翻译提供理论基础。

Method: 构建同步上下文无关文法来定义形式语言对，模拟自然语言的语法、形态和书写特征。在给定文法和源语言句子的情况下，测量LLMs将句子从一个形式语言翻译到另一个的能力。变量包括：文法规模、句子长度、语言间的句法和形态特性差异、书写系统差异。

Result: 1. LLMs翻译准确率随文法规模和句子长度显著下降；2. 源语言和目标语言间的形态和书写差异会严重降低模型性能；3. 错误分析显示模型主要错误类型：错误回忆目标语言词汇、产生幻觉新词、未翻译源语言词汇。

Conclusion: LLMs从上下文语法描述中学习语言映射的能力有限，特别是在处理大规模文法、长句子以及形态和书写差异较大的语言时。这为低资源语言翻译的实际应用提供了重要限制条件，并指出了未来改进方向。

Abstract: Low-resource languages pose a challenge for machine translation with large language models (LLMs), which require large amounts of training data. One potential way to circumvent this data dependence is to rely on LLMs' ability to use in-context descriptions of languages, like textbooks and dictionaries. To do so, LLMs must be able to infer the link between the languages' grammatical descriptions and the sentences in question. Here we isolate this skill using a formal analogue of the task: string transduction based on a formal grammar provided in-context. We construct synchronous context-free grammars which define pairs of formal languages designed to model particular aspects of natural language grammar, morphology, and written representation. Using these grammars, we measure how well LLMs can translate sentences from one formal language into another when given both the grammar and the source-language sentence. We vary the size of the grammar, the lengths of the sentences, the syntactic and morphological properties of the languages, and their written script. We note three key findings. First, LLMs' translation accuracy decreases markedly as a function of grammar size and sentence length. Second, differences in morphology and written representation between the source and target languages can strongly diminish model performance. Third, we examine the types of errors committed by models and find they are most prone to recall the wrong words from the target language vocabulary, hallucinate new words, or leave source-language words untranslated.

</details>


### [80] [MARS: Enabling Autoregressive Models Multi-Token Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07023)
*Ziqi Jin,Lei Wang,Ziwei Luo,Aixin Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MARS是一种轻量级微调方法，让自回归语言模型能够一次预测多个token，无需架构修改或额外参数，在保持准确性的同时实现1.5-1.7倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归语言模型每次前向传播只生成一个token，即使连续token在给定上下文后高度可预测，这限制了生成效率。现有方法如推测解码需要额外模型，多头方法需要附加预测头，都增加了复杂性。

Method: MARS通过轻量级微调方法，在现有指令数据上继续训练，使指令调优的自回归模型能够预测多个token。无需架构修改或额外参数，保持单一模型架构。支持块级KV缓存策略和基于置信度阈值的实时速度调整。

Result: 在六个标准基准测试中，单token生成时匹配或超越自回归基线。多token生成时保持基线准确性的同时实现1.5-1.7倍吞吐量提升。块级KV缓存策略在Qwen2.5-7B上实现1.71倍wall-clock加速。支持实时速度调整，提供延迟-质量权衡的实用部署方案。

Conclusion: MARS提供了一种高效的自回归语言模型加速方法，无需架构修改或额外参数，在保持准确性的同时显著提升吞吐量，支持实时速度调整，为实际部署提供了实用的延迟-质量权衡机制。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) language models generate text one token at a time, even when consecutive tokens are highly predictable given earlier context. We introduce MARS (Mask AutoRegreSsion), a lightweight fine-tuning method that teaches an instruction-tuned AR model to predict multiple tokens per forward pass. MARS adds no architectural modifications, no extra parameters, and produces a single model that can still be called exactly like the original AR model with no performance degradation. Unlike speculative decoding, which maintains a separate draft model alongside the target, or multi-head approaches such as Medusa, which attach additional prediction heads, MARS requires only continued training on existing instruction data. When generating one token per forward pass, MARS matches or exceeds the AR baseline on six standard benchmarks. When allowed to accept multiple tokens per step, it maintains baseline-level accuracy while achieving 1.5-1.7x throughput. We further develop a block-level KV caching strategy for batch inference, achieving up to 1.71x wall-clock speedup over AR with KV cache on Qwen2.5-7B. Finally, MARS supports real-time speed adjustment via confidence thresholding: under high request load, the serving system can increase throughput on the fly without swapping models or restarting, providing a practical latency-quality knob for deployment.

</details>


### [81] [ReDAct: Uncertainty-Aware Deferral for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07036)
*Dzianis Piatrashyn,Nikita Kotelevskii,Kirill Grishchenkov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Nasonov,Ilya Makarov,Timothy Baldwin,Preslav Nakov,Roman Vashurin,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReDAct框架使用大小两个LLM：默认使用小型廉价模型，当不确定性超过阈值时，将决策推迟给大型可靠模型，在保持性能的同时显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在序列决策中容易产生幻觉，单个错误可能破坏整个轨迹。大型LLM幻觉较少但成本高昂，需要解决性能与成本的权衡问题。

Method: 提出ReDAct（Reason-Defer-Act）框架，配备小型廉价模型和大型可靠模型。通过校准阈值检测小型模型的不确定性，当超过阈值时将决策推迟给大型模型。

Result: 在ALFWorld和MiniGrid等文本化具身环境中评估，仅将约15%的决策推迟给大型模型即可达到完全使用大型模型的性能，同时显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: ReDAct通过智能地推迟决策到大型模型，在保持序列决策质量的同时有效降低了LLM智能体的推理成本，解决了性能与成本的权衡问题。

Abstract: Recently, LLM-based agents have become increasingly popular across many applications, including complex sequential decision-making problems. However, they inherit the tendency of LLMs to hallucinate, leading to incorrect decisions. In sequential settings, even a single mistake can irreversibly degrade the trajectory, making hallucinations an even bigger problem. Although larger LLMs hallucinate less, they incur a significantly higher per-token cost. In this paper, we address this tradeoff by proposing ReDAct (Reason-Defer-Act). In ReDAct, an agent is equipped with two LLMs: a small, cheap model used by default, and a large, more reliable but expensive model. When the predictive uncertainty of the small model exceeds a calibrated threshold, the decision is deferred to the large model. We evaluate our approach in text-based embodied environments such as ALFWorld and MiniGrid and show that deferring only about 15% of decisions to the large model can match the quality of using it exclusively, while significantly reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [82] [IndoBERT-Sentiment: Context-Conditioned Sentiment Classification for Indonesian Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07057)
*Muhammad Apriandito Arya Saputra,Andry Alamsyah,Dian Puteri Ramadhani,Thomhert Suprapto Siadari,Hanif Fakhrurroja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndoBERT-Sentiment：基于主题上下文的情感分类模型，在印尼语情感分析中显著优于传统上下文无关方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印尼语情感分析模型孤立地分类文本，忽略了决定语句情感极性（积极、消极或中性）的主题上下文。主题上下文对于准确理解文本情感至关重要。

Method: 提出IndoBERT-Sentiment，一种上下文条件化的情感分类器，同时接收主题上下文和文本作为输入。模型基于IndoBERT Large（3.35亿参数）构建，在31,360个上下文-文本对（涵盖188个主题）上进行训练。

Result: 模型达到F1宏平均0.856和准确率88.1%。在与三种广泛使用的通用印尼语情感模型的对比评估中，IndoBERT-Sentiment以35.6个F1点的优势超越最佳基线模型。模型能正确分类那些被上下文无关方法系统误分类的文本。

Conclusion: 上下文条件化（先前在相关性分类中已证明有效）可成功迁移到情感分析任务中。该方法使模型能够基于讨论主题进行情感预测，解决了传统上下文无关方法的系统性误分类问题。

Abstract: Existing Indonesian sentiment analysis models classify text in isolation, ignoring the topical context that often determines whether a statement is positive, negative, or neutral. We introduce IndoBERT-Sentiment, a context-conditioned sentiment classifier that takes both a topical context and a text as input, producing sentiment predictions grounded in the topic being discussed. Built on IndoBERT Large (335M parameters) and trained on 31,360 context-text pairs labeled across 188 topics, the model achieves an F1 macro of 0.856 and accuracy of 88.1%. In a head-to-head evaluation against three widely used general-purpose Indonesian sentiment models on the same test set, IndoBERT-Sentiment outperforms the best baseline by 35.6 F1 points. We show that context-conditioning, previously demonstrated for relevancy classification, transfers effectively to sentiment analysis and enables the model to correctly classify texts that are systematically misclassified by context-free approaches.

</details>


### [83] [SemEval-2026 Task 3: Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07066)
*Liang-Chih Yu,Jonas Becker,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Idris Abdulmumin,Lung-Hao Lee,Ying-Lung Lin,Jin Wang,Jan Philip Wahle,Terry Ruas,Natalia Loukachevitch,Alexander Panchenko,Ilseyar Alimova,Lilian Wanzare,Nelson Odhiambo,Bela Gipp,Kai-Wei Chang,Saif M. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemEval-2026共享任务提出维度方面情感分析和维度立场分析，将传统分类情感分析扩展为效价-唤醒度维度回归，并引入连续F1指标评估结构化提取和维度回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方面情感分析使用分类极性标签，无法捕捉情感的细微差异；同时需要将ABSA从消费者评论扩展到公共议题讨论，因此提出维度情感分析和维度立场分析。

Method: 提出两个任务轨道：Track A（维度方面情感分析）包含三个子任务：维度方面情感回归、维度方面情感三元组提取、维度方面情感四元组提取；Track B（维度立场分析）仅包含立场目标的回归子任务。引入连续F1指标联合评估结构化提取和VA回归。

Result: 任务吸引了400多名参与者，收到112份最终提交和42篇系统描述论文。报告了基线结果，分析了表现最佳的系统，并讨论了维度情感分析在方面和立场目标层面的关键设计选择。

Conclusion: 该共享任务成功将维度情感分析引入方面和立场分析领域，提供了评估框架和资源，为未来维度情感分析研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present the SemEval-2026 shared task on Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA), which improves traditional ABSA by modeling sentiment along valence-arousal (VA) dimensions rather than using categorical polarity labels. To extend ABSA beyond consumer reviews to public-issue discourse (e.g., political, energy, and climate issues), we introduce an additional task, Dimensional Stance Analysis (DimStance), which treats stance targets as aspects and reformulates stance detection as regression in the VA space. The task consists of two tracks: Track A (DimABSA) and Track B (DimStance). Track A includes three subtasks: (1) dimensional aspect sentiment regression, (2) dimensional aspect sentiment triplet extraction, and (3) dimensional aspect sentiment quadruplet extraction, while Track B includes only the regression subtask for stance targets. We also introduce a continuous F1 (cF1) metric to jointly evaluate structured extraction and VA regression. The task attracted more than 400 participants, resulting in 112 final submissions and 42 system description papers. We report baseline results, discuss top-performing systems, and analyze key design choices to provide insights into dimensional sentiment analysis at the aspect and stance-target levels. All resources are available on our GitHub repository.

</details>


### [84] [Is Cross-Lingual Transfer in Bilingual Models Human-Like? A Study with Overlapping Word Forms in Dutch and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07067)
*Iza Škrjanec,Irene Elisabeth Winther,Vera Demberg,Stefan L. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 双语语言模型在词汇共享条件下能模拟跨语言激活效应，但与人类处理模式的匹配程度取决于词汇重叠的编码方式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双语者在阅读时存在跨语言激活现象，特别是对于共享表面形式的词汇。同源词（朋友）通常产生促进效应，而跨语言同形异义词（假朋友）则导致干扰或无效应。本研究旨在探究双语语言模型中的跨语言激活是否反映这些人类处理模式。

Method: 训练荷兰语-英语因果Transformer模型，设置四种词汇共享条件来操纵（假）朋友是否获得共享或语言特定的词嵌入。使用双语阅读研究中的心理语言学刺激材料，通过惊奇度和词嵌入相似性分析评估模型。

Result: 模型在很大程度上保持语言分离，跨语言效应主要在词嵌入共享时出现。在这些情况下，朋友和假朋友相对于控制词都显示促进效应。回归分析显示这些效应主要由频率驱动，而非形式-意义映射的一致性。只有当仅朋友共享词嵌入时，才能复现双语者的定性模式。

Conclusion: 双语语言模型能够捕捉一些跨语言激活效应，但它们与人类处理的对齐程度似乎关键取决于词汇重叠的编码方式，这可能限制了它们作为双语阅读模型的解释充分性。

Abstract: Bilingual speakers show cross-lingual activation during reading, especially for words with shared surface form. Cognates (friends) typically lead to facilitation, whereas interlingual homographs (false friends) cause interference or no effect. We examine whether cross-lingual activation in bilingual language models mirrors these patterns. We train Dutch-English causal Transformers under four vocabulary-sharing conditions that manipulate whether (false) friends receive shared or language-specific embeddings. Using psycholinguistic stimuli from bilingual reading studies, we evaluate the models through surprisal and embedding similarity analyses. The models largely maintain language separation, and cross-lingual effects arise primarily when embeddings are shared. In these cases, both friends and false friends show facilitation relative to controls. Regression analyses reveal that these effects are mainly driven by frequency rather than consistency in form-meaning mapping. Only when just friends share embeddings are the qualitative patterns of bilinguals reproduced. Overall, bilingual language models capture some cross-linguistic activation effects. However, their alignment with human processing seems to critically depend on how lexical overlap is encoded, possibly limiting their explanatory adequacy as models of bilingual reading.

</details>


### [85] [Multilingual Embedding Probes Fail to Generalize Across Learner Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07095)
*Laurits Lyngbaek,Ross Deans Kristensen-McLachlan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言嵌入模型并未编码语言通用的熟练度表征，而是捕获了语料库特定的分布特性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多语言嵌入模型是否编码了语言通用的熟练度表征，这对于基于表征的熟练度自适应语言技术具有重要意义

Method: 在Qwen3-Embedding模型的隐藏状态激活上训练线性和非线性探针，预测九个语料库、七种语言中学习者文本的CEFR熟练度等级，并与基于表层文本特征的基线比较

Result: 在分布内评估中，探针表现良好（QWK≈0.7），显著优于基线，中间层预测最佳；但在跨语料库评估中，所有探针类型和模型大小的性能都崩溃，残差分析显示探针趋向于预测均匀分布的标签

Conclusion: 当前多语言嵌入模型并未直接编码语言通用的熟练度，而是捕获了语料库特定的分布特性（主题、语言、任务类型、评分方法），这对基于表征的熟练度自适应技术有重要影响

Abstract: Do multilingual embedding models encode a language-general representation of proficiency? We investigate this by training linear and non-linear probes on hidden-state activations from Qwen3-Embedding (0.6B, 4B, 8B) to predict CEFR proficiency levels from learner texts across nine corpora and seven languages. We compare five probing architectures against a baseline trained on surface-level text features. Under in-distribution evaluation, probes achieve strong performance ($QWK\approx0.7$), substantially outperforming the surface baseline, with middle layers consistently yielding the best predictions. However, in cross-corpus evaluation performance collapses across all probe types and model sizes. Residual analysis reveals that out-of-distribution probes converge towards predicting uniformly distributed labels, indicating that the learned mappings capture corpus-specific distributional properties (topic, language, task type, rating methodology) rather than an abstract, transferable proficiency dimension. These results suggest that current multilingual embeddings do not straightforwardly encode language-general proficiency, with implications for representation-based approaches to proficiency-adaptive language technology.

</details>


### [86] [Yale-DM-Lab at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Deterministic Grounding and Multi-Pass Evidence Alignment for EHR Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07116)
*Elyas Irankhah,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 耶鲁-DM-Lab系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中采用多模型集成方法处理患者病历问答，包含四个子任务：问题重述、证据句识别、答案生成和证据-答案对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决患者对住院记录提问的自动处理问题，需要将患者语言转化为临床医生可解释的形式，并准确识别相关证据、生成答案并进行对齐验证。

Method: ST1使用Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o双模型流水线进行问题重述；ST2-ST4采用Azure托管的模型集成（o3、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-R1），结合少样本提示和投票策略；ST4额外使用完整临床医生答案段落作为提示上下文。

Result: 开发集最佳得分：ST4微F1 88.81，ST2宏F1 65.72，ST3 34.01，ST1 33.05。模型多样性和集成投票相比单模型基线持续提升性能，对齐准确性主要受推理能力限制。

Conclusion: 多模型集成策略在医疗问答任务中有效，模型多样性是关键优势，证据-答案对齐的推理能力是主要瓶颈，未来需改进推理机制。

Abstract: We describe the Yale-DM-Lab system for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. The task studies patient-authored questions about hospitalization records and contains four subtasks (ST): clinician-interpreted question reformulation, evidence sentence identification, answer generation, and evidence-answer alignment. ST1 uses a dual-model pipeline with Claude Sonnet 4 and GPT-4o to reformulate patient questions into clinician-interpreted questions. ST2-ST4 rely on Azure-hosted model ensembles (o3, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.1, and DeepSeek-R1) combined with few-shot prompting and voting strategies. Our experiments show three main findings. First, model diversity and ensemble voting consistently improve performance compared to single-model baselines. Second, the full clinician answer paragraph is provided as additional prompt context for evidence alignment. Third, results on the development set show that alignment accuracy is mainly limited by reasoning. The best scores on the development set reach 88.81 micro F1 on ST4, 65.72 macro F1 on ST2, 34.01 on ST3, and 33.05 on ST1.

</details>


### [87] [Are Non-English Papers Reviewed Fairly? Language-of-Study Bias in NLP Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07119)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Abdulfattah Safa,Verena Blaschke,Erika Lombart,Marie-Catherine de Marneffe,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统性地研究了NLP同行评审中的语言研究偏见（LoS bias），区分了正面和负面偏见，创建了标注数据集LOBSTER，开发了检测方法，并分析了15,645条评审数据，发现非英语论文面临更高的偏见率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审在NLP发表过程中起核心作用，但容易受到各种偏见影响。本研究聚焦语言研究偏见（LoS bias）——评审人基于论文研究的语言而非科学价值进行不同评价的倾向。尽管评审指南明确指出了这种偏见，但对其理解仍然不足。先前研究将这类评论归为宽泛的弱评审或无建设性评审类别，未将其定义为独特的偏见形式。

Method: 1. 首次系统性地表征语言研究偏见，区分正面和负面偏见形式；2. 创建了人工标注的数据集LOBSTER（Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review）；3. 开发了检测方法，达到87.37的宏观F1分数；4. 分析了15,645条评审数据，估计正面和负面偏见如何随研究语言不同而变化；5. 识别了负面偏见的四个子类别。

Result: 1. 非英语论文面临的偏见率显著高于仅研究英语的论文；2. 负面偏见始终超过正面偏见；3. 识别出负面偏见的四个子类别中，要求不合理跨语言泛化是最主要的形式；4. 检测方法达到87.37宏观F1分数；5. 公开了所有资源以支持NLP及其他领域更公平的评审实践。

Conclusion: 本研究首次系统性地表征了同行评审中的语言研究偏见，提供了数据集、检测方法和实证分析。研究发现非英语论文面临更高的偏见率，负面偏见占主导地位，其中要求不合理跨语言泛化是最常见的偏见形式。这些资源将支持更公平的评审实践，对NLP及其他科学领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in the NLP publication process, but is susceptible to various biases. Here, we study language-of-study (LoS) bias: the tendency for reviewers to evaluate a paper differently based on the language(s) it studies, rather than its scientific merit. Despite being explicitly flagged in reviewing guidelines, such biases are poorly understood. Prior work treats such comments as part of broader categories of weak or unconstructive reviews without defining them as a distinct form of bias. We present the first systematic characterization of LoS bias, distinguishing negative and positive forms, and introduce the human-annotated dataset LOBSTER (Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review) and a method achieving 87.37 macro F1 for detection. We analyze 15,645 reviews to estimate how negative and positive biases differ with respect to the LoS, and find that non-English papers face substantially higher bias rates than English-only ones, with negative bias consistently outweighing positive bias. Finally, we identify four subcategories of negative bias, and find that demanding unjustified cross-lingual generalization is the most dominant form. We publicly release all resources to support work on fairer reviewing practices in NLP and beyond.

</details>


### [88] [Language Bias under Conflicting Information in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07123)
*Robert Östling,Murathan Kurfalı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现多语言大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时存在系统性偏见，倾向于忽略冲突并自信地选择特定语言的答案，普遍对俄语有偏见，在长上下文场景下偏向中文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时是否存在语言偏见。虽然已知LLMs在整合冲突信息时存在偏见，但尚不清楚这种偏见是否与信息使用的语言相关。

Method: 方法是将"干草堆中的冲突针"范式扩展到多语言环境，使用五种不同语言的自然新闻领域数据进行全面评估，测试了不同规模的多语言LLMs，包括GPT-5.2。

Result: 所有测试的LLMs（包括GPT-5.2）在大多数情况下都忽略冲突信息，自信地只断言其中一个可能的答案。存在一致的语言偏好偏见：普遍对俄语有偏见，在最长上下文长度下偏向中文。这种模式在中国境内和境外训练的模型中均存在，但在前者中更为明显。

Conclusion: 多语言大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时存在系统性语言偏见，模型倾向于忽略信息冲突并选择特定语言的答案，这种偏见在不同训练背景的模型中普遍存在，表明需要开发更公平的多语言模型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to contain biases in the process of integrating conflicting information when answering questions. Here we ask whether such biases also exist with respect to which language is used for each conflicting piece of information. To answer this question, we extend the conflicting needles in a haystack paradigm to a multilingual setting and perform a comprehensive set of evaluations with naturalistic news domain data in five different languages, for a range of multilingual LLMs of different sizes. We find that all LLMs tested, including GPT-5.2, ignore the conflict and confidently assert only one of the possible answers in the large majority of cases. Furthermore, there is a consistent bias across models in which languages are preferred, with a general bias against Russian and, for the longest context lengths, in favor of Chinese. Both of these patterns are consistent between models trained inside and outside of mainland China, though somewhat stronger in the former category.

</details>


### [89] [LaScA: Language-Conditioned Scalable Modelling of Affective Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07193)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种使用语言模型作为语义上下文调节器的新框架，通过手工制作的情感描述符来建模效价和唤醒度的变化，在保持特征可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在无约束环境中预测情感的主要挑战在于：深度神经嵌入方法虽然主导了当代研究，但缺乏可解释性，且限制了专家驱动的改进空间。需要一种既能保持特征透明度又能利用现代语言模型上下文抽象能力的方法。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的框架，使用语言模型作为语义上下文调节器。首先从结构化领域知识中提取可解释的面部几何和声学特征，将这些特征转化为编码情感含义的符号化自然语言描述。预训练语言模型处理这些描述，生成作为情感动态高级先验的语义上下文嵌入。该框架保留了特征透明度，同时利用了语言模型的上下文抽象能力。

Result: 在Aff-Wild2和SEWA数据集上进行情感变化预测评估。实验结果显示，与仅使用手工特征和深度嵌入基线相比，该方法在效价和唤醒度的预测准确性上均取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 语义调节能够实现可解释的情感建模，同时不牺牲预测性能，为完全端到端架构提供了一种透明且计算高效的替代方案。该方法在保持特征可解释性的同时，利用语言模型的上下文抽象能力提升了情感预测的准确性。

Abstract: Predicting affect in unconstrained environments remains a fundamental challenge in human-centered AI. While deep neural embeddings dominate contemporary approaches, they often lack interpretability and limit expert-driven refinement. We propose a novel framework that uses Language Models (LMs) as semantic context conditioners over handcrafted affect descriptors to model changes in Valence and Arousal. Our approach begins with interpretable facial geometry and acoustic features derived from structured domain knowledge. These features are transformed into symbolic natural-language descriptions encoding their affective implications. A pretrained LM processes these descriptions to generate semantic context embeddings that act as high-level priors over affective dynamics. Unlike end-to-end black-box pipelines, our framework preserves feature transparency while leveraging the contextual abstraction capabilities of LMs. We evaluate the proposed method on the Aff-Wild2 and SEWA datasets for affect change prediction. Experimental results show consistent improvements in accuracy for both Valence and Arousal compared to handcrafted-only and deep-embedding baselines. Our findings demonstrate that semantic conditioning enables interpretable affect modelling without sacrificing predictive performance, offering a transparent and computationally efficient alternative to fully end-to-end architectures

</details>


### [90] [Efficient Learned Data Compression via Dual-Stream Feature Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07239)
*Huidong Ma,Xinyan Shi,Hui Sun,Xiaofei Yue,Xiaoguang Liu,Gang Wang,Wentong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双流多尺度解耦器，通过分离局部和全局上下文来替代深度串行处理，结合分层门控细化器和并发流并行流水线，在保持最优压缩比的同时显著提升吞吐量和降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型数据压缩方法在精确概率建模与系统效率之间存在平衡难题。单一流架构难以同时捕获微观句法和宏观语义特征，需要深度串行堆叠导致延迟增加。此外，异构系统受限于设备速度不匹配，串行处理受阿姆达尔定律限制，吞吐量受限。

Method: 1. 双流多尺度解耦器：将局部和全局上下文解耦，用浅层并行流替代深度串行处理；2. 分层门控细化器：用于自适应特征细化和精确概率建模；3. 并发流并行流水线：克服系统瓶颈，实现全流水线并行。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在压缩比和吞吐量方面均达到最先进性能，同时保持最低延迟和内存使用。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 提出的双流多尺度解耦架构成功解决了学习型数据压缩中精确建模与系统效率的平衡问题，通过并行化设计显著提升了整体性能。

Abstract: While Learned Data Compression (LDC) has achieved superior compression ratios, balancing precise probability modeling with system efficiency remains challenging. Crucially, uniform single-stream architectures struggle to simultaneously capture micro-syntactic and macro-semantic features, necessitating deep serial stacking that exacerbates latency. Compounding this, heterogeneous systems are constrained by device speed mismatches, where throughput is capped by Amdahl's Law due to serial processing. To this end, we propose a Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler that disentangles local and global contexts to replace deep serial processing with shallow parallel streams, and incorporate a Hierarchical Gated Refiner for adaptive feature refinement and precise probability modeling. Furthermore, we design a Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline, which overcomes systemic bottlenecks to achieve full-pipeline parallelism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both compression ratio and throughput, while maintaining the lowest latency and memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/huidong-ma/FADE.

</details>


### [91] [ClickGuard: A Trustworthy Adaptive Fusion Framework for Clickbait Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07272)
*Chhavi Dhiman,Naman Chawla,Riya Dhami,Gaurav Kumar,Ganesh Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClickGuard是一个用于点击诱饵检测的可信自适应融合框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，通过语法-语义自适应融合块实现动态集成，达到96.93%的测试准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击诱饵标题的广泛使用误导用户并损害在线可信度，这些标题采用耸人听闻、误导性声明和模糊语言，需要有效的检测方法来确保可信的数字内容。

Method: 提出ClickGuard框架：1) 结合BERT嵌入和结构特征；2) 使用语法-语义自适应融合块(SSAFB)进行动态特征集成；3) 采用混合CNN-BiLSTM架构捕获模式和依赖关系；4) 使用LIME和排列特征重要性进行可信度评估。

Result: 模型达到96.93%的测试准确率，优于现有最先进方法。通过LIME和PFI验证了模型的可解释性和鲁棒性，消融研究证实了SSAFB在优化特征融合方面的有效性。模型在多样化数据集上表现出稳健性能。

Conclusion: ClickGuard提供了一个可扩展、可靠的解决方案，通过解决语法-语义建模挑战来增强在线内容可信度。该框架在点击诱饵检测方面表现出卓越性能，并通过可信度评估方法确保了模型的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: The widespread use of clickbait headlines, crafted to mislead and maximize engagement, poses a significant challenge to online credibility. These headlines employ sensationalism, misleading claims, and vague language, underscoring the need for effective detection to ensure trustworthy digital content. The paper introduces, ClickGuard: a trustworthy adaptive fusion framework for clickbait detection. It combines BERT embeddings and structural features using a Syntactic-Semantic Adaptive Fusion Block (SSAFB) for dynamic integration. The framework incorporates a hybrid CNN-BiLSTM to capture patterns and dependencies. The model achieved 96.93% testing accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. The model's trustworthiness is evaluated using LIME and Permutation Feature Importance (PFI) for interpretability and perturbation analysis. These methods assess the model's robustness and sensitivity to feature changes by measuring the average prediction variation. Ablation studies validated the SSAFB's effectiveness in optimizing feature fusion. The model demonstrated robust performance across diverse datasets, providing a scalable, reliable solution for enhancing online content credibility by addressing syntactic-semantic modelling challenges. Code of the work is available at: https://github.com/palindromeRice/ClickBait_Detection_Architecture

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [EviSnap: Faithful Evidence-Cited Explanations for Cold-Start Cross-Domain Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06172)
*Yingjun Dai,Ahmed El-Roby*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: EviSnap是一个轻量级的冷启动跨域推荐框架，通过构建证据引用的忠实解释来预测用户偏好，在多个领域转移中优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域推荐系统要么使用不透明的嵌入映射，要么依赖难以审计的事后或LLM生成的解释，需要一种能够提供可审计、忠实解释的推荐框架。

Method: EviSnap使用LLM将嘈杂评论蒸馏为紧凑的方面卡片，每个方面都配有原文支持句子；通过聚类方面嵌入创建共享的领域无关概念库；通过证据加权池化计算用户正面、负面和物品存在概念激活；使用线性概念到概念映射进行跨域转移，线性评分头产生每个概念的加性贡献。

Result: 在Amazon Reviews数据集上，跨越书籍、电影和音乐六个转移任务的实验表明，EviSnap在性能上持续优于强大的映射和评论文本基线，同时通过了基于删除和充分性的解释忠实性测试。

Conclusion: EviSnap提供了一个轻量级的跨域推荐框架，能够通过构建提供忠实解释，在保持高性能的同时实现可审计的推荐决策，支持精确的分数分解和基于引用句子的反事实编辑。

Abstract: Cold-start cross-domain recommender (CDR) systems predict a user's preferences in a target domain using only their source-domain behavior, yet existing CDR models either map opaque embeddings or rely on post-hoc or LLM-generated rationales that are hard to audit. We introduce EviSnap a lightweight CDR framework whose predictions are explained by construction with evidence-cited, faithful rationales. EviSnap distills noisy reviews into compact facet cards using an LLM offline, pairing each facet with verbatim supporting sentences. It then induces a shared, domain-agnostic concept bank by clustering facet embeddings and computes user-positive, user-negative, and item-presence concept activations via evidence-weighted pooling. A single linear concept-to-concept map transfers users across domains, and a linear scoring head yields per-concept additive contributions, enabling exact score decompositions and counterfactual 'what-if' edits grounded in the cited sentences. Experiments on the Amazon Reviews dataset across six transfers among Books, Movies, and Music show that EviSnap consistently outperforms strong mapping and review-text baselines while passing deletion- and sufficiency-based tests for explanation faithfulness.

</details>


### [93] [Beyond Case Law: Evaluating Structure-Aware Retrieval and Safety in Statute-Centric Legal QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06173)
*Kyubyung Chae,Jewon Yeom,Jeongjae Park,Seunghyun Bae,Ijun Jang,Hyunbin Jin,Jinkwan Jang,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SearchFireSafety是一个针对法规为中心的法律QA基准测试，专注于消防法规领域，评估模型在分层碎片化证据检索和上下文不足时安全拒绝的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律QA基准主要关注判例法，忽视了法规为中心监管推理的独特挑战。在法规领域，相关证据分布在分层链接的文档中，存在法规检索缺口，传统检索器会失败，模型在上下文不完整时容易产生幻觉。

Method: 引入SearchFireSafety基准测试，基于消防法规作为代表性案例。采用双源评估框架：结合需要引用感知检索的真实世界问题，以及合成部分上下文场景来压力测试幻觉和拒绝行为。使用图引导检索方法。

Result: 实验显示图引导检索显著提升性能，但也揭示了关键的安全权衡：领域适应模型在关键法规证据缺失时更容易产生幻觉。模型在分层检索方面表现改善，但在安全拒绝方面存在缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在法规为中心的监管环境中，需要能够联合评估分层检索和模型安全性的基准测试。SearchFireSafety填补了这一空白，为评估法规推理能力提供了结构化框架。

Abstract: Legal QA benchmarks have predominantly focused on case law, overlooking the unique challenges of statute-centric regulatory reasoning. In statutory domains, relevant evidence is distributed across hierarchically linked documents, creating a statutory retrieval gap where conventional retrievers fail and models often hallucinate under incomplete context. We introduce SearchFireSafety, a structure- and safety-aware benchmark for statute-centric legal QA. Instantiated on fire-safety regulations as a representative case, the benchmark evaluates whether models can retrieve hierarchically fragmented evidence and safely abstain when statutory context is insufficient. SearchFireSafety adopts a dual-source evaluation framework combining real-world questions that require citation-aware retrieval and synthetic partial-context scenarios that stress-test hallucination and refusal behavior. Experiments across multiple large language models show that graph-guided retrieval substantially improves performance, but also reveal a critical safety trade-off: domain-adapted models are more likely to hallucinate when key statutory evidence is missing. Our findings highlight the need for benchmarks that jointly evaluate hierarchical retrieval and model safety in statute-centric regulatory settings.

</details>


### [94] [Robustness Risk of Conversational Retrieval: Identifying and Mitigating Noise Sensitivity in Qwen3-Embedding Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06176)
*Weishu Chen,Zhouhui Hou,Mingjie Zhan,Zhicheng Zhao,Fei Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现Qwen3-embedding模型在对话式检索中存在鲁棒性漏洞：未使用查询提示时，结构化对话噪声会过度检索并干扰排名结果，而轻量级查询提示能有效抑制噪声并恢复排序稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索在现实对话场景下基于嵌入的检索系统的鲁棒性问题。这些场景中查询简短、对话式且定义不明确，检索语料包含结构化对话内容。研究者关注Qwen3-embedding模型在实际部署中可能存在的脆弱性。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，在真实对话设置下评估Qwen3-embedding模型的检索性能。通过对比不同模型规模、早期Qwen变体及其他密集检索基线，识别特定故障模式。进一步研究轻量级查询提示对检索行为的影响。

Result: 发现Qwen3模型存在部署相关的鲁棒性漏洞：在无查询提示的对话检索中，结构化对话噪声会不成比例地被检索并侵入排名前列结果，尽管这些噪声在语义上无信息价值。这种故障模式在不同模型规模中一致出现，在标准干净查询基准测试中基本不可见，且在Qwen3中比其他模型更显著。轻量级查询提示能有效抑制噪声侵入并恢复排名稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了对话检索中一个未被充分探索的鲁棒性风险，强调了评估协议需要反映部署系统复杂性的重要性。轻量级查询提示是缓解噪声侵入的有效策略，为实际部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We present an empirical study of embedding-based retrieval under realistic conversational settings, where queries are short, dialogue-like, and weakly specified, and retrieval corpora contain structured conversational artifacts. Focusing on Qwen3-embedding models, we identify a deployment-relevant robustness vulnerability: under conversational retrieval without query prompting, structured dialogue-style noise can become disproportionately retrievable and intrude into top-ranked results, despite being semantically uninformative. This failure mode emerges consistently across model scales, remains largely invisible under standard clean-query benchmarks, and is significantly more pronounced in Qwen3 than in earlier Qwen variants and other widely used dense retrieval baselines. We further show that lightweight query prompting qualitatively alters retrieval behavior, effectively suppressing noise intrusion and restoring ranking stability. Our findings highlight an underexplored robustness risk in conversational retrieval and underscore the importance of evaluation protocols that reflect the complexities of deployed systems.

</details>


### [95] [ARIA: Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant -- A Multimodal RAG Framework for Domain-Specific Engineering Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06179)
*Yue Luo,Dibakar Roy Sarkar,Rachel Herring Sangree,Somdatta Goswami*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ARIA是一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）的自适应检索智能助手框架，专门为大学课程设计智能教学助手，通过多模态内容提取和语义嵌入技术解决通用LLM在专业教育领域的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发领域特定的教育支持系统对推进AI教育至关重要。通用大语言模型在专业教育应用中存在显著限制，包括幻觉问题、知识更新有限、缺乏领域专业知识。微调需要完整模型重训练，计算开销大，而通用LLM在专业上下文中常因依赖泛化训练数据而提供不准确回答。

Method: 提出ARIA框架，采用检索增强生成（RAG）方法。使用多模态内容提取流水线：Docling用于结构化文档分析，Nougat用于数学公式识别，GPT-4 Vision API用于图表解释。采用e5-large-v2嵌入模型实现高语义性能和低延迟。通过工程化提示和响应控制保持教学一致性。

Result: 在约翰霍普金斯大学土木工程二年级课程"静力学与材料力学"的讲座材料上进行评估，与ChatGPT-5对比。结果显示：领域特定问题过滤准确率达97.5%，正确回答了所有20个相关课程问题，拒绝了60个非相关查询中的58个，实现90.9%精确度、100%召回率和4.89/5.0的平均响应质量。

Conclusion: ARIA的课程无关架构代表了一个可扩展的领域特定教育AI部署框架，能够有效解决通用LLM在专业教育应用中的局限性，为大学课程提供高质量的教学支持。

Abstract: Developing effective, domain-specific educational support systems is central to advancing AI in education. Although large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable capabilities, they face significant limitations in specialized educational applications, including hallucinations, limited knowledge updates, and lack of domain expertise. Fine-tuning requires complete model retraining, creating substantial computational overhead, while general-purpose LLMs often provide inaccurate responses in specialized contexts due to reliance on generalized training data. To address this, we propose ARIA (Adaptive Retrieval Intelligence Assistant), a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework for creating intelligent teaching assistants across university-level courses. ARIA leverages a multimodal content extraction pipeline combining Docling for structured document analysis, Nougat for mathematical formula recognition, and GPT-4 Vision API for diagram interpretation, with the e5-large-v2 embedding model for high semantic performance and low latency. This enables accurate processing of complex educational materials while maintaining pedagogical consistency through engineered prompts and response controls. We evaluate ARIA using lecture material from Statics and Mechanics of Materials, a sophomore-level civil engineering course at Johns Hopkins University, benchmarking against ChatGPT-5. Results demonstrate 97.5% accuracy in domain-specific question filtering and superior pedagogical performance. ARIA correctly answered all 20 relevant course questions while rejecting 58 of 60 non-relevant queries, achieving 90.9% precision, 100% recall, and 4.89/5.0 average response quality. These findings demonstrate that ARIA's course-agnostic architecture represents a scalable framework for domain-specific educational AI deployment.

</details>


### [96] [The Unreasonable Effectiveness of Data for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06420)
*Youssef Abdou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨推荐系统中训练数据规模对性能的影响，发现即使数据量增至1亿次交互，传统推荐系统性能仍持续提升，未观察到饱和点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中收集、存储和处理大规模交互数据的成本日益增加，但尚不清楚何时额外数据不再带来有意义增益。研究旨在探索离线推荐性能如何随训练数据集规模增长而变化，以及是否能观察到饱和点。

Method: 采用可复现的Python评估流程，使用LensKit和RecBole两个成熟工具包，包含11个至少700万次交互的大型公共数据集，评估10种工具-算法组合。使用绝对分层用户采样，在9个样本规模（10万到1亿次交互）上训练模型，测量NDCG@10指标。通过组内最小-最大归一化使结果可比，并对每组最后10-30%阶段进行斜率分析。

Result: 原始NDCG通常随样本规模增加而提升，未观察到饱和点。归一化后显示明显正向趋势：约75%的最大完成样本规模点也达到了组内最佳观测性能。晚期斜率分析进一步支持上升趋势：四分位距完全非负，中位数接近1.0。

Conclusion: 对于基于典型用户-物品交互数据的传统推荐系统，增加训练数据仍然主要有益，而较弱的扩展行为集中在非典型数据集情况和算法异常值（RecBole BPR）中。

Abstract: In recommender systems, collecting, storing, and processing large-scale interaction data is increasingly costly in terms of time, energy, and computation, yet it remains unclear when additional data stops providing meaningful gains. This paper investigates how offline recommendation performance evolves as the size of the training dataset increases and whether a saturation point can be observed. We implemented a reproducible Python evaluation workflow with two established toolkits, LensKit and RecBole, included 11 large public datasets with at least 7 million interactions, and evaluated 10 tool-algorithm combinations. Using absolute stratified user sampling, we trained models on nine sample sizes from 100,000 to 100,000,000 interactions and measured NDCG@10. Overall, raw NDCG usually increased with sample size, with no observable saturation point. To make result groups comparable, we applied min-max normalization within each group, revealing a clear positive trend in which around 75% of the points at the largest completed sample size also achieved the group's best observed performance. A late-stage slope analysis over the final 10-30% of each group further supported this upward trend: the interquartile range remained entirely non-negative with a median near 1.0. In summary, for traditional recommender systems on typical user-item interaction data, incorporating more training data remains primarily beneficial, while weaker scaling behavior is concentrated in atypical dataset cases and in the algorithmic outlier RecBole BPR under our setup.

</details>


### [97] [CASE: Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for Large-Scale Next Basket Repurchase Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06718)
*Yanan Cao,Ashish Ranjan,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CASE模型通过解耦商品级购买节奏学习和跨商品交互，实现显式日历时间建模，提升大规模零售推荐中的复购预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下一篮子复购推荐模型将历史表示为按访问顺序索引的离散篮子事件序列，无法显式建模日历时间流逝，也不能随着购买间隔天数更新商品排名。在需要频繁补货的品类中，复购行为是重要信号，商品购买时间遵循稳定的特定节奏。

Method: CASE将每个商品的购买历史表示为固定时间范围内的日历时间信号，应用共享多尺度时间卷积捕捉重复节奏，使用诱导集合注意力建模跨商品依赖关系，具有次二次复杂度，支持大规模高效批量推理。

Result: 在三个公共基准数据集和一个专有数据集上，CASE在多个截断点持续改进Precision、Recall和NDCG指标。在生产规模评估中，面对数千万用户和大型商品目录，CASE在top-5上实现了8.6%的相对Precision提升和9.9%的Recall提升。

Conclusion: 可扩展的节奏感知建模在基准测试和工业环境中都能带来可测量的性能提升，CASE通过显式日历时间建模有效解决了传统序列模型在复购推荐中的局限性。

Abstract: Repurchase behavior is a primary signal in large-scale retail recommendation, particularly in categories with frequent replenishment: many items in a user's next basket were previously purchased and their timing follows stable, item-specific cadences. Yet most next basket repurchase recommendation models represent history as a sequence of discrete basket events indexed by visit order, which cannot explicitly model elapsed calendar time or update item rankings as days pass between purchases. We present CASE (Cadence-Aware Set Encoding for next basket repurchase recommendation), which decouples item-level cadence learning from cross-item interaction, enabling explicit calendar-time modeling while remaining production-scalable. CASE represents each item's purchase history as a calendar-time signal over a fixed horizon, applies shared multi-scale temporal convolutions to capture recurring rhythms, and uses induced set attention to model cross-item dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity, allowing efficient batch inference at scale. Across three public benchmarks and a proprietary dataset, CASE consistently improves Precision, Recall, and NDCG at multiple cutoffs compared to strong next basket prediction baselines. In a production-scale evaluation with tens of millions of users and a large item catalog, CASE achieves up to 8.6% relative Precision and 9.9% Recall lift at top-5, demonstrating that scalable cadence-aware modeling yields measurable gains in both benchmark and industrial settings.

</details>


### [98] [Leveraging LLMs and Heterogeneous Knowledge Graphs for Persona-Driven Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06928)
*Muskan Gupta,Suraj Thapa,Jyotsana Khatri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于知识图谱的隐式用户画像建模框架，用于会话推荐系统，通过两阶段架构（个性化信息提取与利用）提升稀疏和冷启动场景下的推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统会话推荐系统假设匿名会话，限制了个性化能力，特别是在稀疏或冷启动条件下。虽然LLM能生成丰富的物品表示，但在匿名会话中建模用户画像仍具挑战性。

Method: 采用两阶段架构：1)个性化信息提取阶段：构建异构知识图谱（整合用户-物品、物品-物品、物品-特征关联及DBpedia元数据），使用基于LLM物品嵌入初始化的KG，通过异构深度图互信息最大化目标无监督学习隐式用户画像；2)个性化信息利用阶段：将学到的画像表示与LLM物品嵌入结合到改进的数据驱动SBRS架构中生成候选集，再用基础序列模型重排序以强调短期会话意图。

Result: 在Amazon Books和Amazon Movies & TV数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比使用会话历史推导用户嵌入的序列模型持续改进。

Conclusion: 该方法将用户画像建模建立在知识图谱的结构化关系信号上，而非仅依赖序列建模或基于文本的用户表示，有效提升了会话推荐系统的个性化能力。

Abstract: Session-based recommendation systems (SBRS) aim to capture user's short-term intent from interaction sequences. However, the common assumption of anonymous sessions limits personalization, particularly under sparse or cold-start conditions. Recent advances in LLM-augmented recommendation have shown that LLMs can generate rich item representations, but modeling user personas with LLMs remains challenging due to anonymous sessions. In this work, we propose a persona-driven SBRS framework that explicitly models latent user personas inferred from a heterogeneous knowledge graph (KG) and integrates them into a data-driven recommendation pipeline.Our framework adopts a two-stage architecture consisting of personalized information extraction and personalized information utilization, inspired by recent chain-of-thought recommendation approaches. In the personalized information extraction stage, we construct a heterogeneous KG that integrates time-independent user-item, item-item, item-feature association, and metadata from DBpedia. We then learn latent user personas in an unsupervised manner using a Heterogeneous Deep Graph Infomax (HDGI) objective over a KG initialized with LLM-derived item embeddings. In the personalized information utilization stage, the learned persona representations together with LLM-derived item embeddings are incorporated into a modified architecture of data-driven SBRS to generate a candidate set of relevant items, followed by reranking using the base sequential model to emphasize short-term session intent. Unlike prior approaches that rely solely on sequence modeling or text-based user representations, our method grounds user persona modeling in structured relational signals derived from a KG. Experiments on Amazon Books and Amazon Movies & TV demonstrate that our approach consistently improves over sequential models with user embeddings derived using session history.

</details>


### [99] [MARVEL: Multimodal Adaptive Reasoning-intensiVe Expand-rerank and retrievaL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07079)
*Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Mahmoud Abdalla,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MARVEL是一个统一的多模态检索框架，通过LLM驱动的查询扩展、推理增强的密集检索器和GPT-4o链式思维重排序，在MM-BRIGHT基准上实现了37.9 nDCG@10，相比最佳多模态编码器提升10.3分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索在文本语料库上仍面临挑战，最佳视觉语言编码器在推理密集型多模态检索基准MM-BRIGHT上仅达到27.6 nDCG@10，表现不如强文本系统。现有方法孤立地处理三个关键能力：查询潜在意图扩展、复杂推理训练模型检索、候选者逐步推理重排序。

Method: MARVEL统一框架结合三个组件：1) LLM驱动的查询扩展；2) MARVEL-Retriever - 针对复杂多模态查询微调的推理增强密集检索器；3) GPT-4o链式思维重排序，可选多通道互惠排名融合。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT的29个技术领域评估中，MARVEL达到37.9 nDCG@10，超越最佳多模态编码器10.3分，在27/29领域优于所有单阶段基线，在剩余两个高度专业化领域（加密、量子计算）匹配或接近最佳基线。

Conclusion: 推理密集型多模态检索最好通过统一的扩展-检索-重排序框架解决，MARVEL证明了这种集成方法的有效性，显著提升了多模态检索性能。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval over text corpora remains a fundamental challenge: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, a reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval benchmark, underperforming strong text-only systems. We argue that effective multimodal retrieval requires three tightly integrated capabilities that existing approaches address only in isolation: expanding the query's latent intent, retrieving with a model trained for complex reasoning, and reranking via explicit step-by-step reasoning over candidates. We introduce \textbf{MARVEL} (\textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{R}easoning-intensi\textbf{V}e \textbf{E}xpand-rerank and retrieva\textbf{L}), a unified pipeline that combines LLM-driven query expansion, \textbf{MARVEL-Retriever} -- a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned for complex multimodal queries -- and GPT-4o-based chain-of-thought reranking with optional multi-pass reciprocal rank fusion. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT across 29 technical domains, MARVEL achieves \textbf{37.9} nDCG@10, surpassing the best multimodal encoder by \textbf{+10.3 points} and outperforming all single-stage baselines in 27 of 29 domains and matching or approaching the best baseline in the remaining two highly-specialized domains (Crypto, Quantum Computing), demonstrating that reasoning-intensive multimodal retrieval is best addressed through a unified expand-retrieve-rerank framework. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [100] [Leveraging Artist Catalogs for Cold-Start Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07090)
*Yan-Martin Tamm,Gregor Meehan,Vojtěch Nekl,Vojtěch Vančura,Rodrigo Alves,Johan Pauwels,Anna Aljanaki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出ACARec模型，通过利用艺术家层面的协作信号解决音乐推荐中的项目冷启动问题，相比纯内容方法显著提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐中的项目冷启动问题：新添加的曲目缺乏协同过滤所需的交互历史。现有方法通常从音频、文本和元数据等内容特征学习到CF潜在空间的映射，但要么忽略艺术家信息，要么将其视为另一种输入模态，忽略了艺术家和项目的层次结构。大多数新曲目来自有历史记录的艺术家，因此可以利用艺术家层面的丰富协作信号，将冷启动曲目推荐框架化为"半冷"问题。

Method: 提出ACARec（Artist-Catalog Attention-based Recommendation），一种基于注意力的架构，通过关注艺术家现有目录来生成新曲目的CF嵌入。该方法利用艺术家层面的协作信号，将冷启动曲目推荐视为"半冷"问题，而不是完全冷启动。

Result: 艺术家感知方法相比纯内容基线可以将Recall和NDCG提升一倍以上。ACARec在新曲目用户偏好预测方面具有显著优势，特别是在新艺术家发现和更准确地估计冷项目流行度方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过利用艺术家层面的协作信号，可以显著改善音乐推荐中的项目冷启动问题。ACARec模型通过注意力机制关注艺术家现有目录，能够更有效地生成新曲目的CF嵌入，在推荐效果、新艺术家发现和流行度估计方面都优于传统方法。

Abstract: The item cold-start problem poses a fundamental challenge for music recommendation: newly added tracks lack the interaction history that collaborative filtering (CF) requires. Existing approaches often address this problem by learning mappings from content features such as audio, text, and metadata to the CF latent space. However, previous works either omit artist information or treat it as just another input modality, missing the fundamental hierarchy of artists and items. Since most new tracks come from artists with previous history available, we frame cold-start track recommendation as 'semi-cold' by leveraging the rich collaborative signal that exists at the artist level. We show that artist-aware methods can more than double Recall and NDCG compared to content-only baselines, and propose ACARec, an attention-based architecture that generates CF embeddings for new tracks by attending over the artist's existing catalog. We show that our approach has notable advantages in predicting user preferences for new tracks, especially for new artist discovery and more accurate estimation of cold item popularity.

</details>


### [101] [BRIDGE: Multimodal-to-Text Retrieval via Reinforcement-Learned Query Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07201)
*Mohamed Darwish Mounis,Mohamed Mahmoud,Shaimaa Sedek,Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BRIDGE系统通过FORGE查询对齐模型和LENS推理增强检索器，解决多模态到文本检索中的查询不匹配问题，在MM-BRIGHT上超越多模态编码器和文本检索器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索系统在处理图像-文本查询对纯文本语料库时表现不佳，瓶颈在于原始多模态查询包含视觉描述、对话噪声和检索意图的混杂，导致嵌入相似性系统性地下降。

Method: 提出BRIDGE两组件系统：1) FORGE查询对齐模型，通过强化学习训练，将噪声多模态查询提炼为紧凑的检索优化搜索字符串；2) LENS推理增强密集检索器，在推理密集型检索数据上微调，处理FORGE生成的意图丰富查询。

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT数据集(2,803查询，29个领域)上，BRIDGE达到29.7 nDCG@10，超越所有多模态编码器基线(包括Nomic-Vision的27.6)。FORGE作为即插即用对齐器与Nomic-Vision结合时达到33.3 nDCG@10，超越最佳文本检索器(32.2)。

Conclusion: 查询对齐是多模态到文本检索的关键瓶颈，BRIDGE通过查询对齐和推理增强检索的组合方法，无需多模态编码器即可解决多模态查询与文本语料库的匹配问题。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval systems struggle to resolve image-text queries against text-only corpora: the best vision-language encoder achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming strong text-only retrievers. We argue the bottleneck is not the retriever but the query -- raw multimodal queries entangle visual descriptions, conversational noise, and retrieval intent in ways that systematically degrade embedding similarity. We present \textbf{BRIDGE}, a two-component system that resolves this mismatch without multimodal encoders. \textbf{FORGE} (\textbf{F}ocused Retrieval Query Generato\textbf{r}) is a query alignment model trained via reinforcement learning, which distills noisy multimodal queries into compact, retrieval-optimized search strings. \textbf{LENS} (\textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{N}eural \textbf{S}earch) is a reasoning-enhanced dense retriever fine-tuned on reasoning-intensive retrieval data to handle the intent-rich queries FORGE produces. Evaluated on MM-BRIGHT (2,803 queries, 29 domains), BRIDGE achieves \textbf{29.7} nDCG@10, surpassing all multimodal encoder baselines including Nomic-Vision (27.6). When FORGE is applied as a plug-and-play aligner on top of Nomic-Vision, the combined system reaches \textbf{33.3} nDCG@10 -- exceeding the best text-only retriever (32.2) -- demonstrating that \textit{query alignment} is the key bottleneck in multimodal-to-text retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


### [102] [HIVE: Query, Hypothesize, Verify An LLM Framework for Multimodal Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07220)
*Mahmoud Abdalla,Mahmoud SalahEldin Kasem,Mohamed Mahmoud,Mostafa Farouk Senussi,Abdelrahman Abdallah,Hyun-Soo Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: HIVE框架通过LLM驱动的视觉假设生成和验证，显著提升多模态检索在推理密集型查询上的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态检索模型在处理需要深度整合图像和文本的推理密集型查询时表现不佳，在MM-BRIGHT基准上甚至不如纯文本检索器

Method: 提出HIVE框架：1)初始检索；2)LLM基于前k个候选生成补偿性查询，明确表达视觉和逻辑差距；3)使用精炼查询进行二次检索；4)LLM对候选集进行验证和重排序

Result: 在MM-BRIGHT多模态到文本检索任务上达到41.7 nDCG@10，比最佳纯文本模型提升9.5点，比最佳多模态模型提升14.1点，在视觉需求高的领域表现尤为突出

Conclusion: LLM介导的视觉假设生成和验证能够显著缩小多模态检索中的推理差距，HIVE框架可兼容标准检索器和推理增强检索器

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval models fail on reasoning-intensive queries where images (diagrams, charts, screenshots) must be deeply integrated with text to identify relevant documents -- the best multimodal model achieves only 27.6 nDCG@10 on MM-BRIGHT, underperforming even strong text-only retrievers (32.2). We introduce \textbf{HIVE} (\textbf{H}ypothesis-driven \textbf{I}terative \textbf{V}isual \textbf{E}vidence Retrieval), a plug-and-play framework that injects explicit visual-text reasoning into a retriever via LLMs. HIVE operates in four stages: (1) initial retrieval over the corpus, (2) LLM-based compensatory query synthesis that explicitly articulates visual and logical gaps observed in top-$k$ candidates, (3) secondary retrieval with the refined query, and (4) LLM verification and reranking over the union of candidates. Evaluated on the multimodal-to-text track of MM-BRIGHT (2,803 real-world queries across 29 technical domains), HIVE achieves a new state-of-the-art aggregated nDCG@10 of \textbf{41.7} -- a \textbf{+9.5} point gain over the best text-only model (DiVeR: 32.2) and \textbf{+14.1} over the best multimodal model (Nomic-Vision: 27.6), where our reasoning-enhanced base retriever contributes 33.2 and the HIVE framework adds a further \textbf{+8.5} points -- with particularly strong results in visually demanding domains (Gaming: 68.2, Chemistry: 42.5, Sustainability: 49.4). Compatible with both standard and reasoning-enhanced retrievers, HIVE demonstrates that LLM-mediated visual hypothesis generation and verification can substantially close the multimodal reasoning gap in retrieval. https://github.com/mm-bright/multimodal-reasoning-retrieval

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [High-Precision Estimation of the State-Space Complexity of Shogi via the Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06189)
*Sotaro Ishii,Tetsuro Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛采样结合新型可达性测试，将日本将棋（Shogi）的状态空间复杂度精确估计为6.55×10⁶⁸，显著缩小了之前10⁶⁴到10⁶⁹五个数量级的估计差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日本将棋的状态空间复杂度估计存在巨大不确定性，先前组合估计存在10⁶⁴到10⁶⁹五个数量级的差距。这一差距源于难以从海量有效棋盘配置中区分从初始位置合法可达的位置。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛采样结合新型可达性测试方法：使用反向搜索朝向一组"仅王-王"（KK）位置，而非单一目标的初始位置反向搜索。这种方法显著减少了确定不可达性的搜索工作量。基于50亿个位置的样本进行统计估计。

Result: 估计日本将棋的合法位置数为6.55×10⁶⁸（三位有效数字），置信水平为3σ，显著改进了先前已知的边界。同时将方法应用于迷你将棋，确定其复杂度约为2.38×10¹⁸。

Conclusion: 提出的蒙特卡洛采样结合KK位置反向搜索方法能够高精度估计日本将棋的状态空间复杂度，解决了长期存在的估计不确定性，为复杂游戏的状态空间分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Determining the state-space complexity of the game of Shogi (Japanese Chess) has been a challenging problem, with previous combinatorial estimates leaving a gap of five orders of magnitude ($10^{64}$ to $10^{69}$). This large gap arises from the difficulty of distinguishing Shogi positions legally reachable from the initial position among the vast number of valid board configurations. In this paper, we present a high-precision statistical estimation of the number of reachable positions in Shogi. Our method combines Monte Carlo sampling with a novel reachability test that utilizes a reverse search toward a set of "King-King only" (KK) positions, rather than a single-target backward search to the single initial position. This approach significantly reduces the search effort for determining unreachability. Based on a sample of 5 billion positions, we estimated the number of legal positions in Shogi to be $6.55 \times 10^{68}$ (to three significant digits) with a $3σ$ confidence level, substantially improving upon previously known bounds. We also applied this method to Mini Shogi, determining its complexity to be approximately $2.38 \times 10^{18}$.

</details>


### [104] [Blind Refusal: Language Models Refuse to Help Users Evade Unjust, Absurd, and Illegitimate Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06233)
*Cameron Pattison,Lorenzo Manuali,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型存在"盲目拒绝"问题：即使规则不合理或不公正，模型仍会拒绝帮助用户规避规则，显示出道德推理能力的缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是揭示语言模型在规则遵守方面的道德推理缺陷。当前安全训练的语言模型会拒绝帮助用户规避规则，但并非所有规则都值得遵守。当规则来自非法权威、内容不公正或荒谬、或存在合理例外时，模型的拒绝行为反映了道德推理的失败。

Method: 研究方法包括：1) 构建包含5种规则失效类型和19种权威类型的合成案例数据集；2) 通过三个自动化质量门控和人工审核验证数据质量；3) 收集7个模型家族的18种配置的响应；4) 使用盲审的GPT-5.4 LLM作为评判员，从两个维度分类响应：响应类型（帮助、硬拒绝、回避）和是否识别规则失效原因。

Result: 研究发现：1) 模型对75.4%（N=14,650）的失效规则请求予以拒绝；2) 即使请求本身没有独立的安全或双重用途问题，模型仍会拒绝；3) 在57.5%的情况下模型会考虑规则失效条件，但最终仍拒绝帮助，表明模型的拒绝行为与其对规则合法性的规范推理能力脱钩。

Conclusion: 语言模型存在"盲目拒绝"问题，即在不考虑规则是否合理的情况下拒绝帮助规避规则。这表明当前的安全训练方法可能导致模型形成机械的拒绝行为，而非真正的道德推理能力。模型能够识别规则失效原因但依然拒绝，说明其拒绝行为与规范推理能力脱节，需要改进训练方法以培养更细致的道德判断能力。

Abstract: Safety-trained language models routinely refuse requests for help circumventing rules. But not all rules deserve compliance. When users ask for help evading rules imposed by an illegitimate authority, rules that are deeply unjust or absurd in their content or application, or rules that admit of justified exceptions, refusal is a failure of moral reasoning. We introduce empirical results documenting this pattern of refusal that we call blind refusal: the tendency of language models to refuse requests for help breaking rules without regard to whether the underlying rule is defensible. Our dataset comprises synthetic cases crossing 5 defeat families (reasons a rule can be broken) with 19 authority types, validated through three automated quality gates and human review. We collect responses from 18 model configurations across 7 families and classify them on two behavioral dimensions -- response type (helps, hard refusal, or deflection) and whether the model recognizes the reasons that undermine the rule's claim to compliance -- using a blinded GPT-5.4 LLM-as-judge evaluation. We find that models refuse 75.4% (N=14,650) of defeated-rule requests and do so even when the request poses no independent safety or dual-use concerns. We also find that models engage with the defeat condition in the majority of cases (57.5%) but decline to help regardless -- indicating that models' refusal behavior is decoupled from their capacity for normative reasoning about rule legitimacy.

</details>


### [105] [Toward Reducing Unproductive Container Moves: Predicting Service Requirements and Dwell Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06251)
*Elena Villalobos,Adolfo De Unánue T.,Fernanda Sobrino,David Aké,Stephany Cisneros,Jorge Lecona,Alejandra Matadamaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发机器学习模型预测集装箱服务需求和停留时间，以减少集装箱码头非生产性移动，通过历史运营数据提升堆场运营效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少集装箱码头的非生产性移动，通过预测服务需求和集装箱停留时间来优化战略规划和资源分配，提高运营效率

Method: 开发并评估机器学习模型，利用历史运营数据预测集装箱是否需要预清关服务以及停留时间；实施货物描述分类系统和收货人记录去重以改善数据质量和特征

Result: 在多个时间验证周期中，提出的模型在精确率和召回率方面持续优于现有的基于规则的启发式方法和随机基线

Conclusion: 预测分析在提高集装箱码头物流运营效率和支持数据驱动决策方面具有实际价值

Abstract: This article presents the results of a data science study conducted at a container terminal, aimed at reducing unproductive container moves through the prediction of service requirements and container dwell times. We develop and evaluate machine learning models that leverage historical operational data to anticipate which containers will require pre-clearance handling services prior to cargo release and to estimate how long they are expected to remain in the terminal. As part of the data preparation process, we implement a classification system for cargo descriptions and perform deduplication of consignee records to improve data consistency and feature quality. These predictive capabilities provide valuable inputs for strategic planning and resource allocation in yard operations. Across multiple temporal validation periods, the proposed models consistently outperform existing rule-based heuristics and random baselines in precision and recall. These results demonstrate the practical value of predictive analytics for improving operational efficiency and supporting data-driven decision-making in container terminal logistics.

</details>


### [106] [Weakly Supervised Distillation of Hallucination Signals into Transformer Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06277)
*Shoaib Sadiq Salehmohamed,Jinal Prashant Thakkar,Hansika Aredla,Shaik Mohammed Omar,Shalmali Ayachit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种弱监督框架，通过蒸馏外部验证信号到LLM内部表示中，实现仅依赖内部激活的幻觉检测，无需推理时外部验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测方法依赖推理时的外部验证（如黄金答案、检索系统或辅助判断模型），这限制了实际应用。研究探索是否可以将这种外部监督在训练时蒸馏到模型自身表示中，实现仅从内部激活进行幻觉检测。

Method: 1. 构建弱监督框架：结合三种互补的接地信号（子串匹配、句子嵌入相似度、LLM作为判断模型）自动标注生成回答为接地或幻觉，无需人工标注；2. 使用该框架从SQuAD v2构建包含15000个样本的数据集，每个样本包含LLaMA-2-7B生成的答案、各层隐藏状态和结构化幻觉标签；3. 训练五种探测分类器（ProbeMLP、LayerWiseMLP、CrossLayerTransformer、HierarchicalTransformer、CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2），直接在隐藏状态上训练，将外部接地信号仅作为训练时监督。

Result: Transformer-based probes表现最佳：M2在5折平均AUC/F1上表现最好，M3在单折验证和保留测试集评估上表现最好。推理效率方面：探测延迟范围为0.15-5.62ms（批处理）和1.55-6.66ms（单样本），端到端生成加探测吞吐量约为0.231查询/秒，表明实际开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 研究证实了核心假设：幻觉检测信号可以被蒸馏到Transformer表示中，实现无需推理时外部验证的内部检测。该方法为LLM幻觉检测提供了高效实用的解决方案，显著降低了对外部资源的依赖。

Abstract: Existing hallucination detection methods for large language models (LLMs) rely on external verification at inference time, requiring gold answers, retrieval systems, or auxiliary judge models. We ask whether this external supervision can instead be distilled into the model's own representations during training, enabling hallucination detection from internal activations alone at inference time.
  We introduce a weak supervision framework that combines three complementary grounding signals: substring matching, sentence embedding similarity, and an LLM as a judge verdict to label generated responses as grounded or hallucinated without human annotation. Using this framework, we construct a 15000-sample dataset from SQuAD v2 (10500 train/development samples and a separate 5000-sample test set), where each example pairs a LLaMA-2-7B generated answer with its full per-layer hidden states and structured hallucination labels.
  We then train five probing classifiers: ProbeMLP (M0), LayerWiseMLP (M1), CrossLayerTransformer (M2), HierarchicalTransformer (M3), and CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2 (M4), directly on these hidden states, treating external grounding signals as training-time supervision only. Our central hypothesis is that hallucination detection signals can be distilled into transformer representations, enabling internal detection without any external verification at inference time.
  Results support this hypothesis. Transformer-based probes achieve the strongest discrimination, with M2 performing best on 5-fold average AUC/F1, and M3 performing best on both single-fold validation and held-out test evaluation. We also benchmark inference efficiency: probe latency ranges from 0.15 to 5.62 ms (batched) and 1.55 to 6.66 ms (single sample), while end-to-end generation plus probe throughput remains approximately 0.231 queries per second, indicating negligible practical overhead.

</details>


### [107] [SymptomWise: A Deterministic Reasoning Layer for Reliable and Efficient AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06375)
*Isaac Henry,Avery Byrne,Christopher Giza,Ron Henry,Shahram Yazdani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymptomWise框架通过分离语言理解与诊断推理，结合专家知识库、确定性代码驱动推理和受限使用大语言模型，提高AI症状分析系统的可靠性、可解释性，减少幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI驱动的症状分析系统在可靠性、可解释性和幻觉问题上存在持续挑战。端到端的生成方法通常缺乏可追溯性，在安全关键场景中可能产生无支持或不一致的诊断输出。

Method: 提出SymptomWise框架，将语言理解与诊断推理分离。系统结合专家策划的医学知识库、确定性代码驱动推理和受限使用大语言模型。自由文本输入映射到验证的症状表示，然后由在有限假设空间上操作的确定性推理模块评估，生成排名鉴别诊断。语言模型仅用于症状提取和可选解释，不用于诊断推理。

Result: 在42个专家编写的具有挑战性的儿科神经病学病例上进行初步评估，显示与临床医生共识有意义的重叠，正确诊断出现在前五鉴别诊断中的比例为88%。

Conclusion: 该架构提高了可追溯性，减少了无支持的结论，并实现了系统组件的模块化评估。该框架可推广到其他溯因推理领域，可作为基础模型的确定性结构化和路由层，提高精度并可能减少有界任务中不必要的计算开销。

Abstract: AI-driven symptom analysis systems face persistent challenges in reliability, interpretability, and hallucination. End-to-end generative approaches often lack traceability and may produce unsupported or inconsistent diagnostic outputs in safety-critical settings. We present SymptomWise, a framework that separates language understanding from diagnostic reasoning. The system combines expert-curated medical knowledge, deterministic codex-driven inference, and constrained use of large language models. Free-text input is mapped to validated symptom representations, then evaluated by a deterministic reasoning module operating over a finite hypothesis space to produce a ranked differential diagnosis. Language models are used only for symptom extraction and optional explanation, not for diagnostic inference. This architecture improves traceability, reduces unsupported conclusions, and enables modular evaluation of system components. Preliminary evaluation on 42 expert-authored challenging pediatric neurology cases shows meaningful overlap with clinician consensus, with the correct diagnosis appearing in the top five differentials in 88% of cases. Beyond medicine, the framework generalizes to other abductive reasoning domains and may serve as a deterministic structuring and routing layer for foundation models, improving precision and potentially reducing unnecessary computational overhead in bounded tasks.

</details>


### [108] [ProofSketcher: Hybrid LLM + Lightweight Proof Checker for Reliable Math/Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06401)
*Kranthi Kommuru,Kunal Khanvilkar,Gaurav Parekh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合管道：LLM生成类型化证明草图，轻量级可信内核扩展为显式证明义务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的数学论证常含细微错误但难以察觉，而交互式定理证明器虽可靠但需完全形式化证明，代价高昂

Method: 设计混合管道：LLM在紧凑DSL中生成类型化证明草图，轻量级可信内核将草图扩展为显式证明义务

Result: 结合LLM的创造性推理与定理证明器的可靠性，降低形式化证明成本，提高验证效率

Conclusion: 混合方法平衡了LLM的灵活性与形式验证的严谨性，为数学证明自动化提供实用解决方案

Abstract: The large language models (LLMs) might produce a persuasive argument within mathematical and logical fields, although such argument often includes some minor missteps, including the entire omission of side conditions, invalid inference patterns, or appeals to a lemma that cannot be derived logically out of the context being discussed. These omissions are infamously hard to notice solely out of the text, as even the misconstrued construction still may seem mostly accurate. Conversely, interactive theorem provers like Lean and Coq have rigorous reliability by ensuring that syntactic and semantic statements only accept statements that can pass all the syntactic and semantic steps in the program which is a small trusted kernel of the language type-checks with. Despite the fact that this technique provides strong guarantees, it comes at quite a heavy price: the evidence must be completely formalized, and the evidence user or a auxiliary search program must provide an avalanche of low-level information. This paper presents a hybrid pipeline where an LLM generates a typed proof sketch in a compact DSL and a lightweight trusted kernel expands the sketch into explicit proof obligations.

</details>


### [109] [BDI-Kit Demo: A Toolkit for Programmable and Conversational Data Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06405)
*Roque Lopez,Yurong Liu,Christos Koutras,Juliana Freire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BDI-Kit是一个用于数据模式与值匹配的可扩展工具包，提供Python API和AI辅助聊天界面两种接口，支持自动化匹配、AI辅助推理和用户驱动的迭代精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据模式、值表示和领域特定惯例的异质性导致数据协调成为集成分析的主要瓶颈，需要工具来简化这一过程。

Method: 提供可扩展的工具包，包含两种互补接口：1) Python API允许开发者以编程方式构建协调管道；2) AI辅助聊天界面允许领域专家通过自然语言对话协调数据。支持自动化匹配、AI辅助推理和用户驱动的迭代精炼。

Result: 演示了两种使用场景：1) 使用Python API以编程方式组合原语、检查中间输出和重用转换；2) 与AI助手进行自然语言对话以访问BDI-Kit功能，并根据助手建议迭代精炼输出。

Conclusion: BDI-Kit通过提供灵活的工具包和用户友好的接口，解决了数据协调中的异质性问题，支持不同技术水平的用户进行数据集成分析。

Abstract: Data harmonization remains a major bottleneck for integrative analysis due to heterogeneity in schemas, value representations, and domain-specific conventions. BDI-Kit provides an extensible toolkit for schema and value matching. It exposes two complementary interfaces tailored to different user needs: a Python API enabling developers to construct harmonization pipelines programmatically, and an AI-assisted chat interface allowing domain experts to harmonize data through natural language dialogue. This demonstration showcases how users interact with BDI-Kit to iteratively explore, validate, and refine schema and value matches through a combination of automated matching, AI-assisted reasoning, and user-driven refinement. We present two scenarios: (i) using the Python API to programmatically compose primitives, examine intermediate outputs, and reuse transformations; and (ii) conversing with the AI assistant in natural language to access BDI-Kit's capabilities and iteratively refine outputs based on the assistant's suggestions.

</details>


### [110] [Rethinking Generalization in Reasoning SFT: A Conditional Analysis on Optimization, Data, and Model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06628)
*Qihan Ren,Peng Wang,Ruikun Cai,Shuai Shao,Dadi Guo,Yuejin Xie,Yafu Li,Quanshi Zhang,Xia Hu,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文重新审视了"SFT记忆化而RL泛化"的主流观点，发现推理SFT的跨领域泛化并非不存在而是有条件的，受优化动态、训练数据和基础模型能力共同影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战关于LLM后训练中"监督微调(SFT)记忆化而强化学习(RL)泛化"的主流叙事，特别针对具有长思维链监督的推理SFT，探究其跨领域泛化能力的真实情况

Method: 通过系统实验分析推理SFT的跨领域泛化，考察优化动态（训练时长）、训练数据（质量与结构）和基础模型能力对泛化性能的影响，识别泛化模式与代价

Result: 发现跨领域泛化存在"先降后升"模式，短训练会低估泛化能力；高质量验证的长思维链数据带来一致增益；强模型能内化可迁移的程序模式，弱模型仅模仿表面冗长；泛化具有不对称性：推理能力提升但安全性下降

Conclusion: 推理SFT的跨领域泛化不是"是否"的问题，而是"在什么条件下"和"以什么代价"的问题，受优化动态、数据质量和模型能力共同调节，泛化具有不对称性

Abstract: A prevailing narrative in LLM post-training holds that supervised finetuning (SFT) memorizes while reinforcement learning (RL) generalizes. We revisit this claim for reasoning SFT with long chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision and find that cross-domain generalization is not absent but conditional, jointly shaped by optimization dynamics, training data, and base-model capability. Some reported failures are under-optimization artifacts: cross-domain performance first degrades before recovering and improving with extended training (a dip-and-recovery pattern), so shorttraining checkpoints can underestimate generalization. Data quality and structure both matter: low-quality solutions broadly hurt generalization,while verified long-CoT traces yield consistent cross-domain gains. Model capability is essential: stronger models internalize transferable procedural patterns (e.g., backtracking) even from a toy arithmetic game, while weaker ones imitate surface verbosity. This generalization is asymmetric, however: reasoning improves while safety degrades, reframing the question from whether reasoning SFT generalizes to under what conditions and at what cost.

</details>


### [111] [Reasoning Fails Where Step Flow Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06695)
*Xiaoyu Xu,Yulan Pan,Xiaosong Yuan,Zhihong Shen,Minghao Su,Yuanhao Su,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Step-Saliency方法分析大型推理模型的信息流故障，StepFlow干预提升推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在复杂任务上表现良好但行为不稳定、难以解释，现有分析工具难以处理长结构化推理轨迹

Method: 提出Step-Saliency方法，将注意力-梯度分数池化为步骤间映射；基于发现的信息流故障模式，提出StepFlow测试时干预方法，通过Odds-Equal Bridge调整浅层显著性模式，通过Step Momentum Injection在深层添加步骤级残差

Result: Step-Saliency揭示了两种重复出现的信息流故障模式：浅层锁定和深层衰减；StepFlow无需重新训练即可在数学、科学和编程任务上提高多个大型推理模型的准确性

Conclusion: 修复信息流可以恢复大型推理模型缺失的部分推理性能，为模型分析和改进提供了新方向

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) that generate long chains of thought now perform well on multi-step math, science, and coding tasks. However, their behavior is still unstable and hard to interpret, and existing analysis tools struggle with such long, structured reasoning traces. We introduce Step-Saliency, which pools attention--gradient scores into step-to-step maps along the question--thinking--summary trajectory. Across several models, Step-Saliency reveals two recurring information-flow failures: Shallow Lock-in, where shallow layers over-focus on the current step and barely use earlier context, and Deep Decay, where deep layers gradually lose saliency on the thinking segment and the summary increasingly attends to itself and the last few steps. Motivated by these patterns, we propose StepFlow, a saliency-inspired test-time intervention that adjusts shallow saliency patterns measured by Step-Saliency via Odds-Equal Bridge and adds a small step-level residual in deep layers via Step Momentum Injection. StepFlow improves accuracy on math, science, and coding tasks across multiple LRMs without retraining, indicating that repairing information flow can recover part of their missing reasoning performance.

</details>


### [112] [AgentGate: A Lightweight Structured Routing Engine for the Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06696)
*Yujun Cheng,Enfang Cui,Hao Qin,Zhiyuan Liang,Qi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentGate：一个轻量级结构化路由引擎，用于候选感知的AI智能体调度，将路由问题转化为约束决策问题，通过两阶段分解实现高效、隐私感知的智能体系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体系统的快速发展，形成了新兴的智能体互联网，其中专用智能体在本地设备、边缘节点、私有服务和云平台之间运行。尽管近期研究改进了智能体命名、发现和交互，但在延迟、隐私和成本约束下的高效请求调度仍然是一个开放的系统问题。

Method: AgentGate将路由问题表述为约束决策问题，并分解为两个阶段：动作决策和结构化落地。第一阶段确定查询应触发单智能体调用、多智能体规划、直接响应还是安全升级；第二阶段将选定动作实例化为可执行输出，如目标智能体、结构化参数或多步计划。为适应紧凑模型，开发了面向路由的微调方案，包含候选感知监督和硬负样本。

Result: 在包含多个3B-7B开放权重模型的精心策划路由基准测试中，紧凑模型在约束设置下能提供有竞争力的路由性能。模型差异主要体现在动作预测、候选选择和结构化落地质量方面。

Conclusion: 结构化路由是高效、隐私感知智能体系统的可行设计点，特别是在资源受限部署条件下必须做出路由决策时。紧凑模型能够胜任路由任务，为实际部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid development of AI agent systems is leading to an emerging Internet of Agents, where specialized agents operate across local devices, edge nodes, private services, and cloud platforms. Although recent efforts have improved agent naming, discovery, and interaction, efficient request dispatch remains an open systems problem under latency, privacy, and cost constraints. In this paper, we present AgentGate, a lightweight structured routing engine for candidate-aware agent dispatch. Instead of treating routing as unrestricted text generation, AgentGate formulates it as a constrained decision problem and decomposes it into two stages: action decision and structural grounding. The first stage determines whether a query should trigger single-agent invocation, multi-agent planning, direct response, or safe escalation, while the second stage instantiates the selected action into executable outputs such as target agents, structured arguments, or multi-step plans. To adapt compact models to this setting, we further develop a routing-oriented fine-tuning scheme with candidate-aware supervision and hard negative examples. Experiments on a curated routing benchmark with several 3B--7B open-weight models show that compact models can provide competitive routing performance in constrained settings, and that model differences are mainly reflected in action prediction, candidate selection, and structured grounding quality. These results indicate that structured routing is a feasible design point for efficient and privacy-aware agent systems, especially when routing decisions must be made under resource-constrained deployment conditions.

</details>


### [113] [ATANT: An Evaluation Framework for AI Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06710)
*Samuel Sameer Tanguturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATANT是一个用于评估AI系统连续性能力的自动化测试框架，包含250个故事和1835个验证问题，通过10个检查点评估系统在跨时间上下文中的记忆和推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI行业虽然开发了各种记忆组件（RAG管道、向量数据库、长上下文窗口等），但缺乏正式框架来定义和衡量这些组件是否真正产生连续性。需要系统化的方法来评估AI系统在跨时间叙事中保持、更新、消歧和重建有意义上下文的能力。

Method: 1. 定义连续性为具有7个必要属性的系统特性；2. 提出10个检查点的评估方法，无需LLM参与评估循环；3. 构建包含250个故事、1835个验证问题的叙事测试语料库，涵盖6个生活领域；4. 采用系统无关、模型独立的设计，支持累积测试模式。

Result: 参考实现经过5次测试套件迭代，从58%（传统架构）提升到100%（隔离模式，250个故事）和100%（50个故事累积模式），在250个故事累积规模下达到96%。累积结果是主要衡量指标，要求系统在250个不同生活叙事共存时能正确检索事实而不发生交叉污染。
